GROUP PROFILE
About Fountain Set (Holdings) Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Fountain Set Group”) (Stock Code:
00420.HK)

One of the world’s largest and long-established fabric manufacturers, the Fountain Set Group produces knitted fabric and garments
through vertically integrated operations in fabric knitting, dyeing, printing, finishing and garment manufacturing, for many of the
renowned apparel retailers and brands.

As a strong believer of research and development, we collaborate with brands and suppliers to develop innovative and creative fabric
and garment products to consumers all over the world. Headquartered in Hong Kong and listed on the Main Board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited, the Fountain Set Group has 7 production facilities in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”),
Sri Lanka and Indonesia, with marketing and representative offices in 4 countries and a global staff force of close to 5,800 strong.

Mr. HA Chung Fong, founder and honorary chairman of the Company remains one of the substantial shareholders of the Company.

To learn more about Fountain Set (Holdings) Limited, please visit www.fshl.com


集團簡介
有關福田實業（集團）有限公司（
              「本公司」
                  ）及其附屬公司（
                         「福田集團」
                              ）（股份代號：00420，香港）

福田集團為一家世界最大及歷史悠久的針織面料生產商之一，提供針織、染色、印花、整理及成衣製造等高度垂直綜合服務，為
許多知名服裝零售商和品牌製造針織面料及成衣。

作為研發的堅信者，我們與品牌和供應商合作，為世界各地的消費者開發創新和創意的面料及成衣產品。福田集團總部位於香
港，並於香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，其於中華人民共和國（
                             「中國」
                                ）、斯里蘭卡及印尼設有7個生產設施，市場推廣辦事
處及代表處分佈4個國家，全球員工接近5,800人。

名譽主席夏松芳先生仍為本公司主要股東之一。

欲知更多福田實業（集團）有限公司資料，請瀏覽www.fshl.com

Dear Shareholders,                                                                  致各股東：

On behalf of the board of directors of Fountain Set (Holdings) Limited (the         本人謹代表福田實業（集團）有限公司董事會（分
“Company” or “we” or “our”, and the “Board”, respectively), I would like to         別指「本公司」或「我們」及「董事會」   ）謹此提呈本
present the audited consolidated results of the Company and its subsidiaries        公司及其附屬公司（   「本集團」或「福田集團」   ）截
(the “Group” or the “Fountain Set Group”) for the financial year ended 31           至2023年12月31日止財政年度（「 2023年」
                                                                                                             ）經審
December 2023 (“2023”), and to provide you with an overview of the Group’s          核之綜合業績，並提供本集團策略及業務展望
strategy and business outlook.                                                      之概覽。

FINANCIAL AND BUSINESS REVIEW                                                       財務及業務回顧
The financial year of 2023 was a highly challenging year for the Group, mainly      2023財年對本集團而言是極具挑戰的一年，主
attributed to (1) the global economic growth slowdown, insufficient international   要由於(1)全球經濟增速放緩，國際市場需求不
market demand, and significant decrease in the average unit price of main           足，印染面料主要產品出口平均單價明顯下降；
exported textile products; (2) inadequate total domestic demand and intense         (2)國內總需求不足，內銷市場價格競爭白熱化；
price competition in the domestic market; (3) a sharp increase in primary           (3)要素成本剛性上漲、江陰福匯紡織有限公司
costs and unresolved relocation issues concerning Jiangyin Fuhui Textiles           （本 公 司 間 接 全 資 附 屬 公 司）
                                                                                                          （「江 陰 福 匯」）拆
Limited (the Company’s indirect wholly-owned subsidiary) (“Jiangyin Fuhui”),        遷事宜仍未得到徹底解決，導致經營壓力增加；
resulting in increased operating pressures; and (4) proactive adjustments to        及(4)主動對江陰福匯產能進行調整，支付若干
Jiangyin Fuhui’s production capacity, including the voluntary payment of one-off    僱員的一次性遣散賠償。
redundancy severance compensation to certain employees.

     In 2016, we started a revolutionary new business – centralised heat supply           自2016年，我們開創一項革新業務─集中供熱
     project (the “Project”), of which the business model is a pioneer in the industry.   項目（ 「項目」  ），其業務模式是業界先驅。沙田鎮
     The Shatian Town Environmental Industrial Park (the “Park”) (for electroplating,     環保工業園（   「工業園」    ）
                                                                                                            （針對著色、面料印花及
     fabric printing and dyeing industry) is one of the seven largest environmental       染色行業）為中國東莞市七大環保工業園之一，
     industrial parks in Dongguan, China. It integrates and assembles the industry’s      匯合及聚集園內及沙田鎮其他地區須搬遷的同
     enterprises within the Park and in other areas in Shatian Town, which are            行業企業。該項目乃該工業園發展的附屬項目
     required to be relocated. The Project, being one of the ancillary projects of the    之一，亦屬廣東工業園及產業集群區集中供熱
     industrial park development, is also a key project of the implementation plan of     實施計劃的關鍵項目。該項目的建成已減少區
     the centralised heat supply in Guangdong Industrial Park and Industrial Cluster      內原有的28座燃煤鍋爐，透過降低氮氧化物、二
     Areas. The Project has been contributing to cleaner air for the community and        氧化硫及煙塵排放量，為社區及周邊地區營造
     neighbourhood by reducing the emission of nitrogen oxide, sulphur dioxide as         更潔淨的空氣環境。該項目已於2018年完成第
     well as smoke and dust by reducing the remaining 28 coal-fired heaters within        一期建設並投產供熱予區內企業，2019年項目
     the district. The Project has completed the first phase of construction in 2018      啟動第二期建設，而根據廣東省「藍天保衛戰」               ，
     and put into operation to supply heat to local enterprises. The second phase         二 期 拆 除 餘 下 的 燃 煤 鍋 爐 後 新 建2台 天 然 氣 鍋
     of construction of the Project started in 2019. According to the Guangdong           爐，已 於2021年 建 成 並 投 產，逐 步 替 代 原 來 的
     Province’s “Blue Sky Defense”, second phase commenced after the removal              燃煤鍋爐。天然氣屬於潔淨能源且能高效燃燒
     of the remaining coal-fired boilers and two new natural gas boilers have been        利用，建成後除能更進一步降低大氣污染物的
     built and put into operation in 2021, gradually replacing the original coal-         排放量，還能減少總體能源消耗量及碳排放量，
     fired boilers. Natural gas is a clean energy source, which can be efficiently        提高資源利用率並促進綠色發展、改善本地區
     burned and utilised. Upon the completion, it can further reduce overall energy       的生態環境面貌，達至提升整體社會效益以及
     consumption and carbon emissions, promote green development, and improve             人民幸福感。
     ecological environment of the region, overall social benefits and contentment.

     The Group will continue to invest in advanced technologies and install               本集團將繼續投資採用先進技術及新增設備以
     additional equipment to prevent and reduce pollution. The Group provides             預防及減少污染。本集團定期向員工提供有關

     Risk Management                                                                    風險管理

     The Group follows a stringent and prudent risk management policy to manage         集團會依據嚴格及審慎的風險管理政策應對外
     foreign exchange and interest rate risks.                                          匯及利率風險。

     The sale and the purchase of raw materials of the Group were mainly                集團之銷售和原料採購以港元、美元及人民幣
     denominated in HKD, USD and RMB. Since the Group mainly carries out                為主。而集團主要的生產基地位於中國，故涉及
     production in China, we are exposed to foreign exchange risk arising from RMB      人民幣所產生之外匯風險。集團一直密切留意
     exposure. The Group has been closely monitoring the exchange rate fluctuation      美元及人民幣匯率的變動，會採取謹慎的方法，
     for USD and RMB and will continue to monitor the trend of the exchange rate        適時審視匯率的走勢，安排合適的措施以減低
     and adopt appropriate measures in order to mitigate the foreign currency risk in   外匯風險。
     a cautious manner.

     In addition, bank borrowings of the Group were denominated in HKD and              此外，集團的銀行借貸以港元為單位，利息主要
     interests were mainly charged on a floating rate basis. The average effective      以浮動利率計算。本集團銀行借貸之平均實際
     interest rate of bank borrowings of the Group was 5.99% per annum (2022:           年利率5.99%（2022：2.64%）
                                                                                                           ，及銀行借貸須於
     2.64% per annum) for the year ended 31 December 2023 and the bank                  兩年內償還（ 2022：三年內）。截至2023年12月
     borrowings were repayable within two years (2022: within three years). All         31日及2022年12月31日止年度，所有銀行借貸
     bank borrowings were unsecured for the years ended 31 December 2023                均為無抵押。本集團將繼續留意市場的利率波
     and 31 December 2022. The Group will continue to monitor the interest rates        動，並安排合適的措施以減低利率風險。
     fluctuation in the market and will adopt appropriate measures to minimise the
     interest rate risk.

STAKEHOLDERS’ ENGAGEMENT                                                           權益人參與
We obtain and understand the views of our stakeholders regularly. This             我們定期搜集及了解權益人的意見。這種溝通
communication provides valuable feedback for our business and assists us           為我們的業務提供了寶貴的反饋意見，並有助
to understand stakeholders’ needs and assess the best way to leverage              我們了解權益人的需求，並評估最佳利用資源
our resources and expertise to contribute to future business and community         和專業知識的方法，以促進未來業務和社區的
development.                                                                       發展。

Across the supply chain, we have taken steps throughout the Year to ensure         在整個供應鏈中，我們於整個年度已採取措施
that we operate responsibly and in the interests of our customers, workforce,      確保我們以負責任的態度經營業務，並符合我
suppliers and other stakeholders.                                                  們客戶、員工、供應商和其他權益人的利益。

Employees perform management, administration and human resources,                  本集團僱員分別履行管理、行政及人力資源、營

     Practices and Conduct of Meetings                                                    會議常規及規則

     Four regular Board meetings at approximately quarterly intervals were held           本年度內，本公司大約每季舉行定期董事會會
     during the Year. Each of the Directors has actively participated in each meeting     議。各董事積極親身、透過電話或視頻形式參與
     in person or via telephone or videoconferencing. Decisions were made by              各會議。董事會會議之決定是以多數票表決。
     majority vote at the Board meetings.

     The dates of 2023 regular Board meetings had been determined in 2022 and             2023年董事會定期會議的舉行日期在2022年內
     any amendments to this schedule were notified to all Directors at least 14 days      已定下，所有日期改動均在最少14天前通知各

     CHAIRMAN AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER                                                主席及行政總裁（

     (THE “CEO”)
     Since 1 April 2023, Mr. LIU Xianfu, the Chairman and an ED, has taken up            自2023年4月1日起，本公司主席及執行董事劉
     the position of CEO of the Company, details of which were set out in the            賢福先生開始兼任本公司行政總裁職務，詳情
     Company’s announcement dated 31 March 2023. This deviates from code                 已載於日期為2023年3月31日之公司公告。此會
     provision C.2.1 of the CG Code, which stipulates that the roles of the chairman     偏離企管守則條文C.2.1的規定，主席與行政總
     and chief executive officer should be separate and should not be performed          裁的角色應有區分，不應由一人同時兼任，但考
     by the same individual. However, having considered the current status of            慮到本集團現時的情況，由同一名人士同時擔
     the Group, vesting the roles of both Chairman and CEO in the same person            任主席及行政總裁兩個職務可以讓本集團更有
     enables the Group to plan its overall strategy more efficiently and achieve the     效率地計劃整體策略及完成預算目標。由於董
     budgeted target. The Board believes that the balance of power and authority         事會由經驗豐富人士及專業人士組 成（包括大
     under this arrangement is adequately ensured by the Board which comprises           多數的非執行董事及獨立非執行董事人數），相
     experienced, professional and high caliber individuals with a sufficient number     信會有足夠能力確保職能及權力在此安排下得
     thereof being INEDs.                                                                到平衡。

     POLICY OF DIVERSITY OF THE BOARD                                                      董事會多樣化政策
     The Company has adopted a policy of diversity of the Board (the “Policy”).            本公司已採納董事會多樣化政策（「本政策」）。
     The criteria adopted by the Nomination Committee in considering whether               提名委員會在考慮有關人士是否適合成為董事
     the relevant personnel are suitable as Directors include their qualifications,        時，基 於 上 市 規 則 及 董 事 會 已 採 納 之 本 政 策，
     experience, expertise, skills, knowledge, ethnicity, age, cultural background and     採納的準則包括其資歷、經驗、專長、技能及知
     so on in view of the provisions of the Listing Rules and the particulars of the       識、種族、年齡及文化背景等。本政策之細節總
     Policy as adopted by the Board are summarised as follows:                             結如下：

     Purposes:                                                                             目的：

     1.   to increase diversity at the level of the Board;                                 1.   增加董事會之多元性；
     2.   to enhance the effectiveness of the Board and the quality of its                 2.   提升董事會之效率及表現質素；及
          performance; and
     3.   to support the attainment of strategic objectives/plan and sustainable and       3.   支持達到本公司之策略目標╱計劃及持續
          balanced development of the Group.                                                    及平衡發展。

     Traditionally, there has been short of female talents in the operation level in the   傳統上，由於行業規範和文化影響，本集團在運
     Group due to industry norm and culture influences. However, the Company               營 層 面 上 一 直 缺 乏 女 性 人 才。然 而，本 公 司 相
     believes the balance of gender in the Board would bring more inspiration to the       信，性別平衡將為董事會帶來更多靈感，並促進
     Board and enhance the overall business strategies development of the Group.           本 集 團 整 體 業 務 策 略 發 展。因 此，本 公 司 已 於
     Hence, the Company has appointed a female talent to join the Board with               2023年12月1日委任一位女性人才加入董事會。
     effect from 1 December 2023. As at 31 December 2023, the Company has                  截至2023年12月31日，本公司的員工性別比例
     a workforce gender ratio of approximately 3:1 (male:female) to avoid a single         為約3：1（男：女）    ，以避免員工性別單一化的情
     gender workforce (including the Directors and senior management) and will             況發生（包括董事及高級管理人員），本公司將
     timely review the gender diversity of the Company’s workforce in accordance           適時根據公司規定及集團的業務發展檢討公司
     with the business development of the Group.                                           員工團隊之性別多元化程度。

     Measurable Objectives                                                                 可計量目標

     The optimum composition of the Board will be based on a range of diversity            最佳的董事會組成將按一系列多元化範疇為基
     perspectives, including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational      準，包括但不限於董事的性別、年齡、文化及教
     background, professional qualifications, regional and industry experience, skills,    育背景、專業資格、地區及行業經驗、技能、知
     knowledge and other experience of the Directors. The ultimate decision will be        識及其他資歷。最終人選將按其長處及可為董
     based on merit and contribution that the selected candidate(s) will bring to the      事會提供的貢獻作決定。
     Board.

     or financial misstatement or loss. The Board is also responsible for evaluating    絕對的保證。董事會亦負責評估及釐定本集團

     RISK MANAGEMENT                                                                          風險管理
     The Group has established a comprehensive risk management framework                      本 集 團 於2016年 開 始 推 行 全 面 風 險 管 理 框 架
     (the “Framework”) since 2016. The Framework is to execute the basic risk                 （「框架」）。該框架是指通過在生產、經營與管
     management measures in various business sectors such as production,                      理的各個環節執行風險管理基本流程，以(i)培育
     operation and management, with the aim to (i) develop a group-wide, open and             集團性，開放及主動的風險管理文化及(ii)為本
     proactive risk management culture, and (ii) safeguard the strategic development          集團實現發展戰略與經營業績的總體目標提供
     and current business operations of the Group.                                            保障為目標。

     The Framework consists of the following concepts:                                        框架包括以下的概念：


                                            The Comprehensive Risk Management Framework
                                                         全面風險管理框架




                                                                   Risk Management
                                                                         Cycle
                                                                     風險管理流程




                                                      Risk Management          Risk Management
                                                         Organisation               Culture
                                                      風險管理組織體系                   風險管理文化




     The Company has also established the guideline for the Framework (the                    本公司亦制定了框架的守則（「守則」）。根據守
     “Guideline”). According to the Guideline, the risk management function of the            則，本集團的風險管理職能由董事會、審核委員
     Group is made up of the Board, the AC, the CEO, senior management, the                   會、行政總裁、高級管理人員、總裁辦公會、內
     CEO Office, the IAD and various subsidiaries of the Company.                             部審計部及本公司各個附屬公司所組成。

     The Framework is a tri-layer system. The first layer is mainly made up of senior         框架是一個3層系統。第1層主要包括高級管理
     management and heads of various functional and operational departments of                人員及本集團不同職能部門和業務部門的主
     the Group, who are the key players for minimising potential risks in advance.            管，他們為事前控制風險的關鍵人物。第2層由
     The second layer consists of the AC, the CEO and the CEO Office, which                   審核委員會、行政總裁及總裁辦公會構成，主要
     is mainly responsible for the organisation, co-ordination and planning of the            負責企業風險管理工作的統籌、組織、協調與規
     corporate risk management works and monitors the progress of the risk                    劃，並對該項工作的開展與落實情況進行監控，
     management works. Thus, the second layer is vital during a risk management               是風險控制危機的關鍵。第3層包含審核委員會
     crisis. The third layer is formed by the AC and the IAD to review and audit              和內部審計部，主要負責對現時營運流程如資
     the existing operation flow such as information system, business flow and                訊系統、業務流程及財務披露等方面進行審計
     financial disclosure. The third layer is often the key to mitigate risk factors in the   及審閱，第3層常作為於風險控制危機事後處理
     aftermath of a risk management crisis.                                                   上減低風險因素的關鍵。

Group’s business, results of operations, financial condition and prospects.            狀況及前景造成重大不利影響。

the Group.

     Credit Risk                                                                         信貸風險

     Credit risk arises from the possibility that a customer in a transaction may        信貸風險源於客戶未能履行其交易承擔。本集
     default. The Group identifies and manages credit risk by defining the target        團透過設定目標市場分部、制定適當的信貸政
     market segment, formulating appropriate credit policies, and carrying out credit    策和執行信貸評核程序，以及監控資產素質，來
     assessment and monitoring asset quality. The Group has established policies,        識別和管理信貸風險。本集團已制訂多項政策、
     procedures, and rating systems to identify, measure, monitor, control and           程序及評級系統，以識別、衡量、監察、控制及
     report on credit risk. They are reviewed and enhanced on an ongoing basis           匯報本集團所承受的信貸風險。本集團持續檢
     to cater for market changes, statutory requirements and best practices in risk      討和改善該等指引，以配合市場轉變、有關法定
     management processes.                                                               要求及風險管理程序的最佳做法。

KEY AUDIT MATTERS (continued)                                                       關鍵審核事項（續）
Impairment of inventories                                                           存貨減值

Refer to notes 4(g) and 19 to the consolidated financial statements                 請參閱綜合財務報表附註4(g)及19

A degree of substandard products is typically produced during the normal            貴集團於日常生產過程中通常會產生一定
course of the Group’s production process. Moreover, inventories of the Group        程 度 的 不 合 格 產 品。此 外，若 無 訂 單 或 取 消
will become aged and/or obsolete in the absence or cancellation of orders. The      訂 單， 貴 集 團 的 存 貨 會 變 為 陳 舊 及 ╱ 或 廢
Group maintains a sophisticated information technology system to keep track         棄。 貴集團設有完善的資訊科技系統，從以監
of the aging and utilisation of inventories and materials regarded as wastage for   察存貨及被視作廢棄物的材料之賬齡及使用情
the estimation of net realisable value.                                             況以估算可變現淨值。

Based on the utilisation of inventories which became wastage and long-aged,         根 據 廢 棄 及 陳 舊 存 貨 的 使 用 情 況， 貴 集 團 於
the Group made an accumulated provision of HK$174,981,000 as at year end            年度終結時經參考存貨的估計可變現淨值作出
by reference to estimated net realisable values of inventories.                     累計撥備174,981,000港元。

We identified the impairment of inventories as a key audit matter because           本核數師行確定存貨減值為關鍵審核事項，此
the estimates of net realisable values and how materials are regarded as            乃由於對有關可變現淨值作出減值估計以及材
wastage on which this impairment entailed a significant degree of management        料為何被視為廢棄材料時，涉及管理層的重大
judgement.                                                                          判斷。

Our response:                                                                       本核數師行的應對：

We performed audit procedures in relation to management’s impairment                本核數師行就管理層的存貨減值評估進行的審
assessment of inventories which included:                                           核程序包括：

–    obtaining an understanding of the key procedures implemented by                －    了解管理層在估計存貨可變現淨值及存
     management in estimating the net realisable values of inventories and               貨撥備以及進行定期審查過時存貨時所
     allowance for inventories and performing periodic reviews of inventory              實施的關鍵程序；
     obsolescence;

     the annual report.

     (“HK$”), which is also the functional currency of the Company.                      為本公司的功能貨幣。

     of its principal subsidiaries are set out in note 35.                               的主要業務載列於附註35內。

Statement 2 – Disclosure of Accounting Policies                                       業準則第2號之修訂本－會計政策的披露的影響
The amendments to HKAS 1 and HKFRS Practice Statement 2 Making                        香 港 會 計 準 則 第1號 及 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 作 業
Materiality Judgements provide guidance and examples to help entities apply           準 則 第2號 之 修 訂 本 作 出 重 大 判 斷 為 實 體 將 重
materiality judgements to accounting policy disclosures. The amendments aim           大判斷應用於會計政策披露提供指引及範例。
to help entities provide accounting policy disclosures that are more useful by        該等修訂本旨在幫助實體提供更為有用的會計
replacing the requirement for entities to disclose their ‘significant’ accounting     政策披露，將實體披露其「重大」會計政策的規
policies with a requirement to disclose their ‘material’ accounting policies and      定取代披露其「重要」會計政策的規定，並加入
adding guidance on how entities apply the concept of materiality in making            有關實體在作出會計政策披露決策時應當如何
decisions about accounting policy disclosures.                                        應用重大性概念的指引。

The application of the amendments has had no material impact on the Group’s           應用該等修訂本並無對本集團的財務表現及狀
financial performance and positions, but has affected the disclosures of              況造成重大影響，惟影響附註4所載會計政策的
accounting policies as set out in note 4.                                             披露。

The amendments to HKAS 8 clarify the distinction between changes in                   香 港 會 計 準 則 第8號 之 修 訂 本 澄 清 了 會 計 估 計
accounting estimates, and changes in accounting policies and the correction of        變動與會計政策變動及更正錯誤之間的區別。
errors. They also clarify how entities use measurement techniques and inputs          其亦闡明了實體如何使用測量技術及輸入數據
to develop accounting estimates.                                                      來制定會計估計。

The amendments had no impact on the consolidated financial statements of              該等修訂本並無對本集團的綜合財務報表造成
the Group.                                                                            任何影響。

Impact on application of Amendments to HKAS 12 - Deferred Tax                         應用香港會計準則第12號之修訂本－與單一交
related to Assets and Liabilities arising from a Single Transaction                   易產生的資產及負債有關的遞延稅項的影響
The amendments to HKAS 12 narrow the scope of the initial recognition                 香港會計準則第12號之修訂本將遞延稅項負債
exemption of deferred tax liabilities and deferred tax assets so that it no longer    及遞延稅項資產之初步確認豁免範圍收窄，使
applies to transactions that, on initial recognition, give rise to equal taxable      其不再適用於初步確認時產生相等的應課稅及
and deductible temporary differences, such as leases and decommissioning              可扣減暫時差額的交易，例如租賃及棄置責任。
liabilities. Therefore, entities are required to recognise a deferred tax asset       因此，實體須就此類交易產生的暫時差額確認
(provided that sufficient taxable profit is available) and a deferred tax liability   遞 延 稅 項 資 產（惟 須 有 充 足 的 應 課 稅 溢 利）及
for temporary differences arising from these transactions. The amendments             遞延稅項負債。該等修訂本應用於與所呈列最
shall be applied to transactions related to leases and decommissioning                早比較期間開始時的租賃及棄置責任有關的交
obligations at the beginning of the earliest comparative period presented, with       易，其中任何累計影響確認為對該日期滾存盈
any cumulative effect recognised as an adjustment to the opening balance of           利或其他權益組成部分（如適合）期初結餘的調
retained earnings or other component of equity as appropriate at that date. For       整。就所有其他交易而言，該等修訂本應用於所
all other transactions, the amendments are applied to those transactions that         呈列最早期間開始後發生的交易。
occur after the beginning of the earliest period presented.

statements of the Group.                                                              重大影響。

     New HKICPA guidance on the accounting implications of the abolition              有關取消強制性公積金（「強積金」）－長期服務
     of the mandatory provident fund (“MPF”) – long service payment                   金（「長服金」）的會計影響的新香港會計師公會
     (“LSP”)                                                                          指引
     In June 2022, the Hong Kong SAR Government (the “Government”) gazetted           於2022年6月，香港特別行政區政府（        「政府」 ）於
     the Hong Kong Employment and Retirement Schemes Legislation (Offsetting          憲報刊登香港2022年僱傭及退休計劃法例（抵
     Arrangement) (Amendment) Ordinance 2022 (the “Amendment Ordinance”),             銷安排）  （修訂）條例（「修訂條例」），該條例將
     which will come into effect from 1 May 2025 (the “Transition Date”). Once the    於2025年5月1日（「過渡日期」）生效。於修訂條
     Amendment Ordinance takes effect, an employer can no longer use any of the       例生效後，僱主不可再使用其強積金計劃的強
     accrued benefits derived from its mandatory contributions to MPF scheme to       制性供款所產生之任何累算權益，扣減僱員由
     reduce the LSP in respect of an employee’s service from the Transition Date      過 渡 日 期 起 服 務 年 期 的 長 服 金（取 消「對 沖 機
     (the abolition of the “offsetting mechanism”). In addition, the LSP in respect   制」）。此外，於過渡日期前的服務年期的長服金
     of the service before the Transition Date will be calculated based on the        將根據僱員於緊接過渡日期前的月薪及截至該
     employee’s monthly salary immediately before the Transition Date and the years   日期的服務年期計算。
     of service up to that date.

     In July 2023, the HKICPA published the “Accounting implications of the           於2023年7月，香港會計師公會發佈「香港取消
     abolition of the MPF-LSP offsetting mechanism in Hong Kong” that provides        強積金－長服金對沖機制的會計影響」，就對沖
     accounting guidance relating to the offsetting mechanism and the abolition of    機制及取消該機制提供會計指引。
     the mechanism.

     The Group has considered the accrued benefits derived from mandatory             本集團將強制性強積金供款產生的累算權益
     MPF contributions that are expected to be used to reduce the LSP payable         （預 期 用 以 扣 減 應 付 予 僱 員 的 長 服 金）視 為 僱
     to an employee as deemed contributions by that employee towards the LSP.         員就長服金的視作供款。一直以來，本集團採用
     Historically, the Group accounted for the offsetting mechanism by applying the   香港會計準則第19.93(b)條可行權宜方法對對沖
     practical expedient in HKAS 19.93(b). Based on the HKICPA guidance, upon         機制進行會計處理。根據香港會計師公會指引，
     the enactment of the Amendment Ordinance in June 2022, these deemed              於修訂條例於2022年6月頒佈後，該等視作供款
     contributions should be attributed to periods of service in the same manner as   乃按與香港會計準則第19.93(a)條應用的長服金
     the gross LSP benefit applying HKAS 19.93(a).                                    總權益相同的方式歸屬於服務期間。

     The Group has assessed the implications of the above HKICPA guidance on          本集團已評估上述香港會計師公會指引對上述
     the above accounting policies and has decided to change those accounting         會計政策的影響，並決定變更該等會計政策，以
     policies to conform with the HKICPA guidance. The management has                 符合香港會計師公會指引。管理層已開始實施
     commenced the processes on implementing the change including additional          變更的程序，包括額外資料收集及影響評估。因
     data collection and impact assessment. It has concluded that the adoption        此，採納香港會計師公會指引及應用新訂或經
     of the HKICPA guidance and the application of the new or amendments to           修訂準則不大可能對本集團當前或以往期間的
     standards is unlikely to have a material impact on the Group’s results and       業績及財務狀況造成重大影響。
     financial position for the current or prior periods.

     Amendments to HKAS 1 Classification of Liabilities as Current or Non-                      香港會計準則第1號之修訂本，流動或非流動負
     current and related amendments to Hong Kong Interpretation 5 (2020);                       債分類及香港詮釋第5號（2020年）
                                                                                                                 ；香港會計準
     Amendments to HKAS 1 – Non-current Liabilities with Covenants                              則第1號之修訂本－附帶契諾之非流動負債

     Amendments to HKAS 1 Classification of Liabilities as Current or Non-                      於2020年頒佈的香港會計準則第1號之修訂本
     current issued in 2020 (the “2020 Amendments”) clarify the requirements on                 流動或非流動負債分類「 2020年修訂本」）闡明
     determining if a liability is current or non-current, in particular the determination      有關釐定負債為流動或非流動負債的規定，尤
     over whether an entity has the right to defer settlement of the liability for at           其是釐定實體是否有權將清償負債的時間推遲
     least 12 months after the reporting period. The amendments specify that an                 至 報 告 期 間 後 至 少12個 月。該 等 修 訂 本 規 定，
     entity’s right to defer settlement must exist at the end of the reporting period.          實體推遲清償的權利須於報告期結束時方可作
     Classification is unaffected by management’s intentions or expectations about              實。分類不受管理層對實體是否行使其推遲清

      (ii)   Impairment loss on financial assets                                           (ii)   金融資產之減值損失

      The Group recognises loss allowances for expected credit loss (“ECL”) on trade       本集團就營業及票據應收款項、其他應收款項
      and bills receivables, other receivables and other financial assets measured at      及按攤銷成本計量的其他金融資產確認預期信
      amortised cost. The ECLs are measured on either of the following bases: (1)          貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）虧損撥備。預期信貸
      12-month ECLs: these are the ECLs that result from possible default events           虧損按以下其中一項基準計量：(1)12個月的預
      within the 12 months after the reporting date; and (2) lifetime ECLs: these are      期信貸虧損：其為於報告日期後12個月內發生
      ECLs that result from all possible default events over the expected life of a        的潛在違約事件導致的預期信貸虧損；及(2)年
      financial instrument. The maximum period considered when estimating ECLs             限內預期信貸虧損：此乃於金融工具預計年期
      is the maximum contractual period over which the Group is exposed to credit          內所有可能由違約事件產生的預期信貸虧損。
      risk.                                                                                估計預期信貸虧損時所考慮的最長期間為本集
                                                                                           團面對信貸風險的最長合約期間。

      ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses. Credit losses are         預期信貸虧損為信貸虧損的概率加權估計。信
      measured as the difference between all contractual cash flows that are due           貸虧損乃按本集團根據合約應收的所有合約現
      to the Group in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that the         金流量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間
      Group expects to receive. The shortfall is then discounted at an approximation       的差額計量。該差額其後按貼近資產原有實際
      to the assets’ original effective interest rate.                                     利率貼現。

The Group has elected to measure loss allowances for trade and bills                 本 集 團 已 選 用 香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第9號 簡 化 法

based on lifetime ECLs. The Group has established a provision matrix that is         根據年限內預期信貸虧損計算預期信貸虧損。
based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-        本集團已設立根據本集團過往信貸虧損經驗計

For other financial assets of the Group, the ECLs are based on the 12-month          就本集團之其他金融資產而言，預期信貸虧損
ECLs. However, when there has been a significant increase in credit risk since       乃以12個月預期信貸虧損為基準。然而，當信貸
origination, the allowance will be based on the lifetime ECLs.                       風險自開始以來大幅增加時，撥備將以年限內
                                                                                     預期信貸虧損為基準。

When determining whether the credit risk of a financial asset has increased          於釐定金融資產之信貸風險自初步確認後有否
significantly since initial recognition and when estimating ECL, the Group           大幅增加及於估計預期信貸虧損時，本集團會
considers reasonable and supportable information that is relevant and available      考慮相關及無須付出過多成本或努力即可獲得
without undue cost or effort. This includes both quantitative and qualitative        之合理及可靠資料。此包括根據本集團之過往
information analysis, based on the Group’s historical experience and informed        經驗及已知信貸評估得出之量化及質化資料及
credit assessment and including forward-looking information.                         分析，並包括前瞻性資料。

The Group assumes that the credit risk on a financial asset has increased            本集團假設，倘金融資產逾期超過30日，其信貸
significantly if it is more than 30 days past due unless the Group has               風險已大幅增加，除非本集團有合理及可靠資
reasonable and supportable information that demonstrate otherwise.                   料證明並非如此。

The Group considers a financial asset to be credit-impaired when: (1) the            本集團認為金融資產於下列情況下為信貸減
borrower is unlikely to pay its credit obligations to the Group in full, without     值：(1)借款人不大可能在本集團無追索權採取
recourse by the Group to actions such as realising security (if any is held); or     行動（例如：變現抵押） （如持有）的情況下向本
(2) the financial asset is more than 365 days past due unless the Group has          集團悉數支付其信貸義務；或(2)該金融資產逾
reasonable and supportable information demonstrate that aware lagging default        期超過365日，除非本集團有合理及可靠資料證
criterion is more appropriate.                                                       明按意識滯後違約標準處理更為恰當。

Depending on the nature of the financial instruments, the assessment of              根據金融工具的性質，對信貸風險顯著增加的
a significant increase in credit risk is performed on either an individual or a      評估乃按個別或共同基準開展。按共同基準開
collective basis. When the assessment is performed on a collective basis, the        展評估時，金融工具根據共有的信貸風險特徵
financial instruments are grouped based on shared credit risk characteristics,       （如逾期狀況及信貸風險評級）進行分組。
such as past due status and credit risk ratings.

The Group considers a financial asset to be credit-impaired when:                    本集團認為金融資產於下列情況下為信貸減
                                                                                     值：

•      significant financial difficulty of the debtor;                               •      債務人出現嚴重財務困難；
•      a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;                    •      違反合約，例如拖欠或逾期事件；
•      the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual reasons relating   •      借款人的貸款人因有關借款人財務困難的
       to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having granted to the borrower a             經濟或合約理由而向借款人授出貸款人原
       concession(s) that the lender(s) would not otherwise consider;                       本不予考慮的優惠；

       financial reorganisation; or                                                         組；或
•      the disappearance of an active market for a security because of financial     •      因出現財務困難導致證券失去活躍市場。
       difficulties.

      borrowings are subsequently measured at amortised cost, using the effective              營公司款項及銀行借貸，其後乃按實際利率法

commitments rather for investment or other purposes.

      (l)   Provisions and contingent liabilities                                            (l)   撥備及或然負債

      Provisions are recognised for liabilities of uncertain timing or amount when the       倘本集團因過往事件而承擔法律或推定責任，
      Group has a legal or constructive obligation arising as a result of a past event,      而有可能將導致經濟利益外流及能夠可靠估計
      which it is probable will result in an outflow of economic benefits that can be        時，則會為未確定時間或金額之負債確認撥備。
      reliably estimated.

      Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic benefits will be required,        倘經濟利益需要外流的可能性不大，或不能對
      or the amount cannot be estimated reliably, the obligation is disclosed as a           有關金額作出可靠估計時，則有關責任會作為
      contingent liability, unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is        或然負債披露，惟經濟利益外流之可能性極低
      remote. Possible obligations, the existence of which will only be confirmed            則作別論。僅以發生或未發生一項或多項未來
      by the occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future events, are also             事件方可確定是否存在的可能責任，亦會披露
      disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the probability of outflow of economic      為或然負債，惟經濟利益外流之可能性極低則
      benefits is remote.                                                                    作別論。

      If the recoverable amount (i.e. the greater of the fair value less costs of disposal   若估計一項資產的可收回金額（即公平價值減
      and value in use) of an asset is estimated to be less than its carrying amount,        處置成本與使用價值兩者的較高者）低於其賬
      the carrying amount of the asset is reduced to its recoverable amount. An              面值時，該資產的賬面值會減至其可收回金額。
      impairment loss is recognised as an expense immediately.                               減值損失即時確認為開支。

      Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the carrying amount of                 倘減值損失其後出現撥回，則該資產的賬面值
      the asset is increased to the revised estimate of its recoverable amount, to           可增加至其經修訂的估計可收回金額，惟增加
      the extent that the increased carrying amount does not exceed the carrying             後的賬面值不得超過該資產於過往年度並未確
      amount that would have been determined had no impairment loss been                     認減值損失時的賬面值。此項減值損失撥回會
      recognised for the asset in prior years. A reversal of an impairment loss is           立即確認為收入，惟有關資產根據另一香港財
      recognised as income immediately, unless the relevant asset is carried at a            務報告準則以重估金額列賬，則減值損失撥回
      revalued amount under another HKFRS, in which case the reversal of the                 會根據該香港財務報告準則視為重估增值。
      impairment loss is treated as a revaluation increase under that HKFRS.

      Value in use is based on the estimated future cash flows expected to be                使用價值按基於預計將源自資產之估計未來現
      derived from the asset, discounted to their present value using a pre-tax              金流量，按照能反映當時市場對貨幣時間值和
      discount rate that reflects current market assessments of the time value of            資產或現金產生單位特定風險的評估的稅前貼
      money and the risks specific to the asset or cash generating unit.                     現率貼現至其現值計算。

a point in time when the customer obtains control of the goods or services.           時間點確認。

(q) Income taxes                                                                     (q) 所得稅

Income taxes for the year comprise current tax and deferred tax.                     是年度所得稅包括是年度稅項及遞延稅項。

Current tax is based on the profit or loss from ordinary activities adjusted for     是年度稅項乃根據日常業務之盈利或虧損，就
items that are non-assessable or disallowable for income tax purposes and is         所得稅而言毋須課稅或不可扣稅之項目作出調
calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively enacted at        整，按報告期終已頒佈或實際上已頒佈的稅率
the end of reporting period.                                                         計算。

Deferred tax is recognised in respect of temporary differences between the           遞延稅項乃就用於財務報告目的之資產及負債
carrying amounts of assets and liabilities for financial reporting purposes          的賬面值與用於稅務目的之相應金額之間的
and the corresponding amounts used for tax purposes. Except for goodwill             暫時差額而確認。除商譽以及不影響會計或應
and recognised assets and liabilities that affect neither accounting nor             課稅盈利的已確認資產及負債外，會就所有應
taxable profits, deferred tax liabilities are recognised for all taxable temporary   課稅暫時差額確認遞延稅項負債。遞延稅項資
differences. Deferred tax assets are recognised to the extent that it is probable    產於有可能動用應課稅盈利抵銷可抵扣暫時差
that taxable profits will be available against which deductible temporary            額的情況下確認。遞延稅項乃按預期資產或負
differences can be utilised. Deferred tax is measured at the tax rates               債變現或清償資產或負債賬面值的方式以及於
appropriate to the expected manner in which the carrying amount of the asset         報告期終已頒佈或實質上已頒佈的適用稅率計
or liability is realised or settled and that have been enacted or substantively      量。
enacted at the end of reporting period.

An exception to the general requirement on determining the appropriate tax           對於計量遞延稅項金額時用於釐定適合稅率的
rate used in measuring deferred tax amount is when an investment property            一般規定而言，有一個例外情況，即投資物業是
is carried at fair value under HKAS 40 “Investment Property”. Unless the             根據香港會計準則第40號「投資物業」而按公平
presumption is rebutted, the deferred tax amounts on these investment                價值列賬。除非該假定被推翻，否則此等投資物
properties are measured using the tax rates that would apply on sale of these        業的遞延稅項金額是以出售此等投資物業時所
investment properties at their carrying amounts at the reporting date. The           適用的稅率以及按其於報告日期的賬面值而計
presumption is rebutted when the investment property is depreciable and is           量。若投資物業是可計提折舊並根據一個商業
held within a business model whose objective is to consume substantially all         模式而持有，而該商業模式的目的是隨著時間
the economic benefits embodied in the property over time, rather than through        推移而消耗該物業所體現的絕大部分經濟效益
sale.                                                                                （而不是通過出售）時，該假定即被推翻。

Estimated allowance for inventories                                                 估計存貨撥備

Inventories are valued at the lower of cost and net realisable value. The           存貨乃按成本及可變現淨值的較低者估值。本
Group regularly reviews its inventory levels in order to identify slow-moving       集團定期檢討其存貨水平，以辨別滯銷及陳舊
and obsolete inventories. The Group makes allowance for slow-moving and             存貨。本集團通過管理層主要基於最新發票價
obsolete items through management’s estimation of the net realisable value          格及當前市況（包括考慮對其後銷售訂單的潛
for such obsolete and slow-moving items based primarily on the latest invoice       在影響及因疫情反覆、全球通脹飆升及全球經
prices and current market conditions, including the consideration of potential      濟的不穩定性導致對產品的需求）對有關陳舊
impacts on the subsequent sales orders and demand of its products resulting         及滯銷項目的可變現淨值估計，就滯銷及陳舊
from the resurgence of the pandemic, soaring global inflation and uncertainties     項目作出撥備。倘本集團發現存貨項目的市價
in the global economy. When the Group identifies items of inventories which         低於其賬面值，則本集團估計存貨撇減的金額
have a market price that is lower than its carrying amount, the Group estimates     為存貨撥備。倘本集團的存貨市價之後低於其
the amount of write-down of inventories as allowance for inventories. If the        賬面值，則或須作出額外撥備。
market price of inventories of the Group becomes lower than its carrying
amount subsequently, an additional allowance may be required.

performance assessment. No analysis on segment assets and liabilities is                                             有作出分析披露，因該資料沒有向主要營運決
disclosed as it is not presented to the chief operating decision makers.                                             策者呈列。

13. INVESTMENT PROPERTIES (continued)                                                   13. 投資物業（續）
Direct comparison method is based on the principle of substitution, where               直接比較法乃以替代原則為基準，而比較乃按
comparison is made based on prices realised on actual sales of comparable               可比較物業實際銷售實現之價格作出。大小、規
properties. Comparable properties of similar size, scale, nature, character             模、性質、特徵及地點相若之可供比較物業乃按
and location are analysed and carefully weighed against all the respective              照各物業之相關利弊進行分析及仔細衡量，以
advantages and disadvantages of each property in order to arrive at a fair              達致公平市值比較。
comparison of market value.

Term and reversion analysis of investment approach estimates the capital value          投資期限和回歸分析法乃在考慮到現有租約的
of a property by capitalised rental income on a fully leased basis having regard        當期租金收入和市場水平的潛在復歸租金收入
to the current passing rental income from existing tenancy and the potential            的基礎上，按全額租賃基準將租金收入資本化
reversionary rental income at market level.                                             估計一項物業的資本價值。

The valuation of leased pier was determined by Depreciated Replacement Cost             租賃碼頭的估值乃按折舊重置成本法釐定，該
approach, which is based on an estimate of the market value for the existing            方法乃根據土地的現有用途對市值作出估計，
use of the land, plus the current cost of replacement of the improvements less          加上目前裝修重置的成本減去實質損耗撥備及
allowance for physical deterioration and all relevant forms of obsolescence and         所有相關形式的陳舊及優化計算。折舊重置成
optimisation. The key input of Depreciated Replacement Cost approach is the             本法的主要輸入數據為每平方米的建築成本。
building construction cost per square metre. A significant increase/decrease in         每平方米的建築成本大幅上升╱下跌將導致租
building construction cost per metre used would result in a significant increase/       賃碼頭之公平價值大幅上升╱下跌。
decrease in the fair value of the leased pier.

The valuation of leased properties takes into account the characteristics of the        租賃物業估值時會考慮投資物業之整體特性，
investment properties, which include the location, size, shape, view, floor level,      包括位置、尺寸、形狀、景觀、樓層、落成年份及
year of completion and other factors collectively, to arrive at the unit market         其他因素，以得出單位市場價格。直接比較法之
price. The key input of direct comparison method is the unit market price. A            主要輸入數據為單位市場價格。市場價格大幅
significant increase/decrease in the market price would result in a significant         上升╱下跌將導致租賃物業之公平價值大幅上
increase/decrease in the fair value of the leased properties.                           升╱下跌。

Significant unobservable inputs were used in the valuation of investment                重大不可觀察輸入參數計量乃用於投資物業之
properties. The fair value of investment properties is a level 3 recurring fair value   估值。投資物業之公平價值為香港財務報告準
measurement under the three-level fair value hierarchy as defined in HKFRS              則第13號公平價值計量所界定的3級公平價值
13, Fair value measurement. There has been no change from the valuation                 層級項下之第3級經常性公平價值計量。於過往
technique used in the prior year. The fair value measurement is based on the            年度採用之估值技術概無變動。公平價值計量
above properties’ highest and best use, which does not differ from their actual         乃按以上述物業之最高及最佳用途為依據，其
use.                                                                                    與物業之實際用途並無差別。

The above investment properties are depreciated on a straight-line basis over           上述投資物業以直線法按20至50年或租賃年期
the shorter of 20 to 50 years or period of the lease term.                              （以較短者為準）折舊。

All of the investment properties of the Group are held to earn rentals or for           本集團所有投資物業乃持作賺取租金或資本增
capital appreciation.                                                                   值。

      27. LEASE LIABILITIES                                                               27. 租賃負債
      Nature of leasing activities (in the capacity as lessee)                            租賃業務之性質（作為承租人）

      The Group leases a number of properties in the jurisdictions from which it          本集團於其營運之司法地區租賃多項物業。本
      operates. The Group leases various factories, staff quarters, offices premises,     集團租賃多間工廠、員工宿舍、辦公室物業、倉
      warehouse and machinery and equipment. Rental contracts are typically made          庫以及機器及設備。租賃合約一般按固定年期2
      for fixed periods from 2 to 50 years for land and buildings and 2 years for         至50年（就土地及建築物而言）及2年（就機器而
      machinery. All rental contracts comprise only fixed payments over the lease         言）訂立。所有租賃合約僅包含於租賃期作出固
      terms.                                                                              定付款。

      Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease components. The Group                合約可包含租賃及非租賃組成部分。本集團根
      allocates the consideration in the contract to the lease and non-lease              據租賃及非租賃組成部分之相對單獨價格，將
      components based on their relative stand-alone prices.                              合約之代價分配至租賃及非租賃組成部分。

      Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and contain a wide range          租賃期乃按個別基準進行磋商，且包含多種不
      of different terms and conditions. The lease agreements do not impose               同條款及條件。租賃協議不會施加任何契諾。租
      any covenants. Leased assets may not be used as security for borrowing              賃資產不得用作借款之抵押。
      purposes.

      The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the lease.    租賃款項乃使用租賃隱含之利率貼現。倘無法
      If that rate cannot be readily determined, which is generally the case for leases   輕易釐定該利率（本集團之租賃通常如此），則
      in the Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used, being the rate       使用承租人之增量借款利率，即個別承租人於
      that the individual lessee would have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to       類似經濟環境根據類似條款、抵押及條件為借
      obtain an asset of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic    入取得與使用權資產價值相若之資產所需之資
      environment with similar terms, security and conditions.                            金而將須支付之利率。

      To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                             為釐定增量借款利率，本集團：

      •    where possible, uses recent third-party financing received by the individual   •   在可能情況下使用個別承租人近期取得之
           lessee as a starting point, adjusted to reflect changes in financing               第三方融資作為基點，並作出調整以反映
           conditions since third party financing was received;                               自取得第三方融資以來融資條件之變動；

      •    uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free interest rate adjusted   •   使用累加法，首先就承租人之信貸風險調
           for credit risk of lessee; and                                                     整無風險利率；及

      •    makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term, country, currency and      •   對租賃作出特定調整，如年期、國家、貨幣
           security.                                                                          及抵押。

                                                                                   並不重大。

      36. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                                36. 資本風險管理
      The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will be able            本集團管理資本之目的，在於確保本集團各實
      to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to stakeholders                 體得以持續經營，同時透過取得債務與權益之
      through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall               間的最佳平衡，為持份者爭取最大回報。本集團
      strategy remains unchanged from prior years.                                               的整體策略與往年一致。

      The capital structure of the Group consists of debts, which include the                    本集團的資本架構包括債務（當中包括附註30
      borrowings disclosed in note 30, and equity attributable to owners of the                  所披露的借貸）及本公司擁有人應佔權益（包括
      Company, comprising issued share capital and reserves.                                     已發行股本及儲備）。

      The directors of the Company review the capital structure on an on-going                   本公司董事按持續基準檢討資本架構。作為檢
      basis. As part of this review, the directors of the Company consider the                   討一部分，本公司董事考慮資本的成本與股本
      cost of capital and the risks associated with the share capital. Based on                  的相關風險。根據本公司董事的建議，本集團會
      recommendations of the directors of the Company, the Group may balance its                 以透過派發股息、發行新股及購回股份以及發
      overall capital structure through the payment of dividends, new share issues               行新債務及贖回現有債務以平衡其整體資本架
      and share buy-backs as well as the issue of new debts and the redemption of                構。
      existing debts.

      The Group monitors capital using a gearing ratio, which is total liabilities divided       本集團使用資本負債比率（即負債總額除以總
      by total capital. Total liabilities is calculated as current liabilities and non-current   資本）監控其資本。負債總額乃按流動負債及非
      liabilities. Capital includes equity attributable to owners of the Company. The            流動負債計算。資本包括本公司擁有人應佔權
      gearing ratio at the end of reporting period was as follows:                               益。於報告期終的資本負債比率如下：

regular review. The Group will continue to assess if the use of the foreign            使用適當外幣對沖策略以降低外幣風險。

      Sensitivity analysis                                                                 敏感度分析

      The Group is mainly exposed to the risk of fluctuation of USD, RMB, CAD, LKR         本集團主要涉及美元、人民幣、加元、盧比及港
      and HK$ when such currencies are different from the functional currency of the       元（當該等貨幣與相關集團實體的功能貨幣不
      relevant group entities.                                                             同時）的波動風險。

      The following table detail the sensitivity of the Group to a 5% increase and         下表詳細載列本集團對上述外幣兌相關集團實
      decrease in the above foreign currencies against the functional currency of          體功能貨幣升值或貶值5%的敏感度，由於港元
      the corresponding group entity, except for HK$ against USD since HK$ is              與美元掛鈎，因此港元兌美元除外。5%為向主
      pegged to USD. 5% is the sensitivity rate used when reporting foreign currency       要管理人員內部呈報外匯風險及管理人員對匯
      risk internally to key management personnel and represents management’s              率可能合理變動的評估所採用的敏感度比率。
      assessment of the reasonably possible change in foreign exchange rates. The          敏感度分析僅包括於年終尚未結算以外幣列

(ii)   Interest rate risk                                                             (ii)   利率風險
The Group is mainly exposed to cash flow interest rate risk primarily in relation     本集團的現金流量利率風險主要與浮息銀行結
to variable-rate bank balances and borrowings. It is the Group’s policy to keep       餘及借貸有關。本集團的政策是維持其銀行借
its bank borrowings at floating rate of interests so as to minimise the fair value    貸於浮動利率以減低公平價值利率風險。本集
interest rate risk. The Group’s cash flow interest rate risk is mainly concentrated   團的現金流量利率風險主要集中於由本集團浮
on the fluctuation of HIBOR or SOFR arising from the Group’s variable-rate            息銀行借貸產生之香港銀行同業拆息或有擔保
bank borrowings. In view of the frequent changes in market interest rate, the         隔夜融資利率波動。由於市場利率頻繁變動，本
Group shall monitor the interest rate fluctuation closely and arrange appropriate     集團會密切留意利率波動，並安排合適的金融
financial instruments to minimise the interest rate risk.                             工具降低利率風險。

      assets as stated in the consolidated statement of financial position.                額。

      monitoring procedures to ensure that follow-up action is taken to recover            程序，以確保採取跟進行動收回逾期債項。產品
      overdue debts. Credit sales of products are only made to customers with good         信貸銷售僅提供予具有良好信貸質素及還款記
      credit quality and repayment history. In addition, the Group regularly reviews       錄 的 客 戶。此 外，本 集 團 會 定 期 按 集 體 及 個 別
      the recoverable amount of debt on both collective and individual basis to            基準檢討債項的可收回款額，以確保就無法收
      ensure that adequate impairment losses are made for irrecoverable amounts.           回款額作出足夠的減值損失。為應對全球經濟

      41. RETIREMENT BENEFIT SCHEMES                                                      41. 退休福利計劃
      The Group operates a defined contribution scheme registered under the               本集團根據職業退休計劃條例註冊成立一項定
      Occupational Retirement Schemes Ordinance (the “ORSO Scheme”) and a                 額供款計劃（「職業退休計劃」）及根據強制性公
      Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the “MPF Scheme”) established under the            積金計劃條例設立一項強制性公積金計劃（「強
      Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme Ordinance for all the eligible employees            積金計劃」）予所有合資格的本集團香港僱員。
      of the Group in Hong Kong. The assets of both schemes are held separately           這兩個計劃的資產乃獨立於本集團資產並由信
      from those of the Group in funds under the control of trustees. The retirement      託人管理。在損益內扣除的退休福利成本乃本
      benefits cost charged to the profit or loss represents the contributions payable    集團根據計劃既定比率計算的應付基金供款。
      to the funds by the Group at rates specified in the rules of the schemes.

      Employees of the Group in other jurisdictions currently participate in state-       本集團其他司法地區的僱員現正參與該司法地
      sponsored pension scheme or defined contribution schemes operated by                區政府營運的國家退休福利計劃或定額供款計
      the Government of such jurisdictions. The Group is required to contribute a         劃。本集團須將僱員薪酬的若干百分比供款予
      certain percentage of their payroll to the schemes to fund the benefits. The only   該等計劃作為福利基金。本集團對該等計劃的
      obligation of the Group with respect to the schemes is to make the required         唯一責任是按規定作出供款。
      contributions.

      During both years, there was no significant forfeited contribution, which arose     於兩個年度，因僱員退出退休福利計劃而沒收
      upon employees leaving the retirement benefits schemes, available to reduce         的供款並不重大，此款項並可用作扣減未來年
      the contribution payable in the future years.                                       度的應付供款。

      The retirement benefits scheme contributions, amounting to HK$58,151,000            已於是年度在損益內扣除的退休福利計劃供款

      42. RELATED AND CONNECTED PARTIES DISCLOSURES                                       42. 關聯及關連人士的披露

      43. EVENTS AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                                              43. 報告期後事項
      For the year ended 31 December 2023, the Group reduced the production              截至2023年12月31日止年度，本集團減少了江
      volume of the fabric mill operated by Jiangyin Fuhui and streamlined the           陰福匯經營的面料工廠的產量，並精簡江陰福
      production team of Jiangyin Fuhui. Taking these circumstances into account         匯的生產團隊。考慮到這些情況並為進一步減
      and to mitigate further losses, the Group has decided to further reduce the        少虧損，本集團決定進一步削減面料產能，同時
      fabric production capacity by solely maintaining fabric knitting production        僅保留江陰福匯營運的面料工廠之織造產能。
      capacity at the fabric mill operated by Jiangyin Fuhui. Details are refer to       詳情請參閱日期為2024年2月5日之公告。
      announcement on 5 February 2024.
